### Needs the owner

See "Rulings of 2026-11-14" near the top of this document: several items below are now decided.

- `packages/finance-engine/src/tax-rules.ts:34-62` `TAX_DISCLAIMER` (`short`, `full`, `mobile`), shown by `TaxDisclaimerButton` in web and by the mobile companion. Not rewritten because it is a notice and states regulatory status ("Flump is not a regulated financial or tax adviser") and liability (7.3, E7). It needs a look: it is a second hand-written disclaimer beside the canonical notices (5.7, R6); it has a Title Case heading with an em dash, em dashes in the body, a sentence of about 90 words, "tax liability" and "self-assessment" in lower case; `full` and `mobile` cite Irish revenue guidance (revenue.ie) in a UK product; and it says all users are assumed to be on England, Wales and Northern Ireland rates, which may no longer be true now that a tax region is saved.
- `packages/mcp/src/assumptions.ts:33` `TAX_NOTICE`. A hand-written tax notice with no "Unverified" in it. Grep finds no caller of `taxVerificationFields` outside tests, so it does not reach a model today. Owner to delete it or fold it into the canonical source (R6, 5.7).
- `packages/mcp/src/tools/read/getTaxYearPosition.ts:190` "Estimate from published UK tax rules and the figures saved in Flump. A calculation, not tax advice." A second disclaimer written by hand in `result.notes`, beside `MCP_NOTICE` in the same result (5.7 says never write a second one). Owner to decide whether it goes.
- `packages/finance-engine/src/tax-year-verification.ts:19` and `:23`, sent as `verification.detail` in `get_tax_year_position`. Verification status wording belongs to the E25 sign-off. Line 19 uses internal terms ("signed-off test vectors", "owner sign-off", "Feature-level verification") and reports `status: "verified"` in a result whose fixed notice says "Unverified", so a model gets two answers. Line 23 is clearer but says "signed off by the owner", which means nothing to a reader.
- The context pack (`formatAiContextPackMarkdown` and the JSON from `GET /mcp-tokens/context-pack`) carries no notice at all, and its tax section has no "Unverified estimate" (R6). The row for line 397 adds the fixed words to the heading as a stopgap; the owner should choose which canonical notice the pack carries.
- The context pack JSON still holds a score and a grade of the person's finances: `healthScore.overallScore`, `healthScore.grade` ("CRITICAL", "NEEDS_ATTENTION", "GOOD", "EXCELLENT"), component `score` and `weight`, `stageEvaluation.canonicalStage` ("STAGE_1_DEBT_RECOVERY", "STAGE_3_WEALTH_OPTIMIZE"), `primaryHeroMetric`, and scoring against Flump-set targets (£1,000, 3 to 6 months, a 4.5% rate, full ISA and pension allowance use). The rows above only remove them from the Markdown and neutralise the text. Removing the fields is an API change, so it is a follow-up (2.3 rules 1 and 6, R4, R3).
- Context pack keys that break the glossary or R7's key rule, for the same follow-up: `taxTrap`, `isInTaxTrap`, `contributionRequiredToEscapeGbp`, `estimatedTaxSavedByEscapingGbp`, `promoCliffAlerts`, `requiredMonthlyToClearBeforeCliff`, `totalAssets`, `totalLiabilities`, `totalLiquidCash`, `discretionarySpendMonthly`, `monthlyDiscretionary`, `monthlySurplus`, `emergencyRunwayMonths`, `weeklyAllowance`, `dailyAllowance` (a per-day figure, 4.1), `snowballFirstWinDescription` ("win"), `topRecommendations` (deprecated and always empty, but `BANNED_KEY_PATTERN` would reject it in an MCP result).
- Facts the context pack states that are defaults, not the person's figures (7.4, 4.4). These need a code fix, not copy: `ai-context-pack.ts:239-246` passes no `highInterestDebtGbp` (so all unsecured debt is labelled high interest), no `cashSavingsYieldApr` (so "3.5% AER" is always reported), no ISA or pension contribution (so the unused ISA allowance always reads £20,000), and passes salary before tax as `adjustedNetIncomeGbp`.
- MCP tool names and result keys that break the glossary. Not renamed here because that is an API change: tool `get_financial_snapshot` ("snapshot"); tools `simulate_debt_payoff` and the `simulate` scope ("simulate"; glossary says "model"); keys `emergencyRunwayMonths` ("runway"), `assetsGbp`, `liabilitiesGbp`, `isLiability`, `liquidCashGbp`, `monthlySurplusAfterStoredMinimumsGbp`, `monthlySurplusBeforeDebtGbp`, `discretionaryMonthlyGbp`, `weightedAprPct`, `consumerDebtGbp`; enum values `avalanche`, `snowball` and parameter `strategy`. Until they change, the proposed descriptions name the key in brackets after the glossary word so a model can map one to the other.
- `packages/mcp/src/tools/read/getTaxYearPosition.ts:195`: for a Scottish taxpayer the tool returns Scottish bands but an England, Wales and Northern Ireland tax estimate, and says so only in a note. The wording is tidied above, but whether to return that estimate at all is a product call (4.4, 7.4).
- `packages/mcp/src/tools/simulate/compareMortgageOverpayVsInvest.ts:52` and `schema.resource.ts:74` say "Deprecated: first tool to be removed from this server." Left as is. Owner to confirm the removal is still planned, since the description states it as fact (7.4).
- `MCP_NOTICE` (`packages/finance-engine/src/disclaimers.ts:13`) is fixed and untouched. One thing for the owner and solicitor to weigh: it ends "Unverified." on every result, including account lists and balance history that are the person's own saved figures, where guide 4.4 rule 3 says saved figures are stated flat.
